# Java UDF bytecode allowlist members, as "<declaring class> <name><descriptor>" (name:descriptor for fields), and the
# classes through which UDF code reaches each declaring class, as "owner <owner class> <declaring class>".
# The extra-insecure prefix marks additions when UDF threads are disabled and allow_extra_insecure_udfs is true.
# Update on Java 11, 17, 21 and 25 with UDFSandboxPolicyTest.main <path of this file>.
